──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n29\t// Types\n30\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n31\t\n32\t/// Result of a single gate validation.\n33\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\n34\tpub enum GateResult {\n35\t    /// The gate passed validation.\n36\t    Pass,\n37\t    /// The gate failed validation with a reason.\n38\t    Fail(String),\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\timpl GateResult {\n42\t    /// Returns true if the gate passed.\n43\t    pub fn passed(&self) -> bool {\n44\t        matches!(self, GateResult::Pass)\n45\t    }\n46\t\n47\t    /// Returns the failure reason if this is a `Fail` result.\n48\t    pub fn failure_reason(&self) -> Option<&str> {\n49\t        match self {\n50\t gates.\n57\t#[derive(Debug)]\n58\tpub struct GateReport {\n59\t    /// Whether all gates passed.\n60\t    pub all_passed: bool,\n61\t    /// Individual gate results keyed by gate name.\n62\t    pub    }\n82\t\n83\t    /// Create a report with a single gate failure.\n84\t    pub fn single_failure(gate_name: impl Into<String>, reason: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n85\t        let mut results = }\n91\t    }\n92\t}\n93\t\n94\t/// Configuration for a single gate from `.needle.yaml`.\n95\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n96\t#[serde(tag = \"type\", rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\n97\tpub enum GateConfig {\n98\t    /// Run shell commands in the workspace directory.\n99\t    Command {\n100\t        commands: Vec<String>,\n101\t        /// Maximum bytes of stderr captured on failure. `None` means \"inherit\n102\t        /// `validation.stderr_cap_bytes`\" — the caller assembling gate configs\n103\t        /// (see occur during bead store validation.\n112\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\n113\tpub enum ValidationError {\n114\t    /// Invalid dependency kind or type.\n115\t    InvalidKind {\n116\t        /// The invalid kind value that was encountered.\n117\t        kind: String,\n118\t    },\n119\t}\n120\t\n121\timpl std::fmt::Display for ValidationError {\n122\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {\n123\t        match self {\n124\t            ValidationError::InvalidKind { kind } => {\n125\t                write!(f, \"invalid kind: '{}'\", kind)\n126\t            }\n127\t        }\n128\t    }\n129\t}\n130\t\n131\timpl std::error::Error for ValidationError {}\n132\t\n133\t/// A single gate command that failed (for backwards compatibility).\n134\t#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n135\tpub struct GateFailure {\n136\t    /// The command that was run.\n137\t    pub command: String,\n138\t    /// Process exit code (None if killed by signal).\n139\t    pub exit_code: Option<i32>,\n140\t    /// Combined stderr output (truncated to a reasonable length).\n141\t    pub output: String,\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\t/// Aggregated result of running a `ValidationGate`.\n145\t#[derive(Debug)]\n146\tpub struct ValidationRunResult {\n147\t    /// Whether all gate commands passed.\n148\t    pub passed: bool,\n149\t    /// List of failures (empty when `passed` is true).\n150\t    pub failures: Vec<GateFailure>,\n151\t}\n152\t\n153\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n154\t// Gate Trait\n155\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n156\t\n157\t/// A validation gate that can check bead work before accepting closure.\n158\t///\n159\t/// Gates are run after an agent exits successfully (code 0). If any gate\n160\t/// returns `GateResult::Fail`, the bead is released instead of having its\n161\t/// closure accepted.\n162\t///\n163\t/// `validate` is async so a slow gate is a genuine `.await` yield point:\n164\t/// `OutcomeHandler::handle_with_cancellation`'s `tokio::time::timeout` can\n165\t/// only preempt a future at such a point (see bf-3saat) — a synchronous,\n166\t/// blocking implementation would let the configured\n167\t/// `validation.outcome_timeout_seconds` observe a slow gate only after the\n168\t/// fact, never actually cut it off.\n169\t#[async_trait::async_trait]\n170\tpub trait Gate: Send + Sync {\n171\t    /// Validate the bead's work in the given workspace.\n172\t    ///\n173\t    /// Returns `GateResult::Pass` if validation succeeds, or `GateResult::Fail`\n174\t    /// with a human-readable reason if it fails.\n175\t    async fn validate(&self, bead: &crate::types::Bead, workspace: &Path) -> Result<GateResult>;\n176\t\n177\t    /// Gate type name for telemetry and configuration (e.g., \"command\", \"custom\").\n178\t    fn gate_type(&self) -> &str;\n179\t}\n180\t\n181\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n182\t// Gate Registry\n183\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n184\t\n185\t/// Registry for pluggable validation gates.\n186\t///\n187\t/// Gates are registered by type name. The registry is thread-safe and supports\n188\t/// dynamic registration of custom gate types.\n189\tpub struct GateRegistry {\n190\t    #[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\n191\t    gates: RwLock<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn Fn(&GateConfig) -> Result<Arc<dyn Gate>> + Send + Sync>>>,\n192\t}\n193\t\n194\timpl Default for GateRegistry {\n195\t    fn default() -> Self {\n196\t        Self::new()\n197\t    }\n198\t}\n199\t\n200\timpl GateRegistry {\n201\t    /// Create a new registry with built-in gate types registered.\n202\t    pub fn new() -> Self {\n203\t        let registry = GateRegistry {\n204\t            gates: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),\n205\t        };\n206\t        // Register built-in gate types\n207\t        registry.register_builtin_gates();\n208\t        registry\n209\t    }\n210\t\n211\t    /// Register a built-in gate type constructor.\n212\t    fn register_builtin_gates(&self) {\n213\t        self.register(\"command\", |config| match config {\n214\t            GateConfig::Command {\n215\t                commands,\n216\t                stderr_cap_bytes,\n217\t            } => Ok(Arc::new(CommandGate::with_stderr_cap(\n218\t                commands.clone(),\n219\t                stderr_cap_bytes.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES),\n220\t            ))),\n221\t        });\n222\t    }\n223\t\n224\t    /// Register a custom gate type constructor.\n225\t    ///\n226\t    /// The constructor function takes a `GateConfig` and returns a boxed `Gate`.\n227\t    /// Custom gates should parse their config from the `GateConfig` enum.\n228\t    ///\n229\t    /// # Example\n230\t    /// ```ignore\n231\t    /// registry.register(\"my_gate\", |config| {\n232\t    ///     // Parse config and create custom gate\n233\t    ///     Ok(Arc::new(MyCustomGate::new(config)?))\n234\t    /// });\n235\t    /// ```\n236\t    pub fn register<F>(&self, gate_type: impl Into<String>, constructor: F)\n237\t    where\n238\t        F: Fn(&GateConfig) -> Result<Arc<dyn Gate>> + Send + Sync + 'static,\n239\t    {\n240\t        let gate_type = gate_type.into();\n241\t        let mut guards = self.gates.write().unwrap();\n242\t        guards.insert(gate_type, Arc::new(constructor));\n243\t    }\n244\t\n245\t    /// Create a gate instance from its configuration.\n246\t    ///\n247\t    /// Returns an error if the gate type is not registered or construction fails.\n248\t    pub fn create_gate(&self, config: &GateConfig) -> Result<Arc<dyn Gate>> {\n249\t        let gate_type = match config {\n250\t            GateConfig::Command { .. } => \"command\",\n251\t        };\n252\t\n253\t        let guards = self.gates.read().unwrap();\n254\t        let constructor = guards\n255\t            .get(gate_type)\n256\t            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"unknown gate type '{}': not registered\", gate_type))?;\n257\t\n258\t        constructor(config)\n259\t    }\n260\t\n261\t    /// Get the global gate registry instance.\n262\t    pub fn global() -> &'static GateRegistry {\n263\t        use std::sync::OnceLock;\n264\t        static REGISTRY: OnceLock<GateRegistry> = OnceLock::new();\n265\t        REGISTRY.get_or_init(GateRegistry::new)\n266\t    }\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n270\t// Built-in Gate Types\n271\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n272\t\n273\t/// Default stderr capture cap (bytes) — preserves the previous hardcoded value.\n274\tconst DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES: usize = 4096;\n275\t\n276\t/// Runs configured shell commands as validation gates.\n277\tpub struct CommandGate {\n278\t    commands: Vec<String>,\n279\t    /// Maximum bytes of stderr captured on failure. Configurable via\n280\t    /// `validation.stderr_cap_bytes` — see GitHub issue jedarden/NEEDLE#9.\n281\t    stderr_cap_bytes: usize,\n282\t}\n283\t\n284\timpl CommandGate {\n285\t    /// Create a new command gate with the default stderr cap (4096 bytes).\n286\t    pub fn new(commands: Vec<String>) -> Self {\n287\t        Self::with_stderr_cap(commands, DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES)\n288\t    }\n289\t\n290\t    /// Create a new command gate with an explicit stderr capture cap.\n291\t    pub fn with_stderr_cap(commands: Vec<String>, stderr_cap_bytes: usize) -> Self {\n292\t        CommandGate {\n293\t            commands,\n294\t            stderr_cap_bytes,\n295\t        }\n296\t    }\n297\t}\n298\t\n299\t#[async_trait::async_trait]\n300\timpl Gate for CommandGate {\n301\t    async fn validate(&self, bead: &crate::types::Bead, workspace: &Path) -> Result<GateResult> {\n302\t        // Run commands sequentially; stop at first failure.\n303\t        for cmd in &self.commands {\n304\t            tracing::info!(\n305\t                command = %cmd,\n306\t                workspace = %workspace.display(),\n307\t                \"running command gate\"\n308\t            );\n309\t\n310\t            match self.run_command(cmd, bead, workspace).await {\n311\t                Ok(()) => {\n312\t                    tracing::info!(command = %cmd, \"command gate passed\");\n313\t                }\n314\t                Err(failure) => {\n315\t                    tracing::warn!(\n316\t                        command = %cmd,\n317\t                        exit_code = ?failure.exit_code,\n318\t                        \"command gate failed\"\n319\t                    );\n320\t                    return Ok(GateResult::Fail(format!(\n321\t                        \"command '{}' failed: {}\",\n322\t                        cmd,\n323\t                        failure.output.trim()\n324\t                    )));\n325\t                }\n326\t            }\n327\t        }\n328\t\n329\t        Ok(GateResult::Pass)\n330\t    }\n331\t\n332\t    fn gate_type(&self) -> &str {\n333\t        \"command\"\n334\t    }\n335\t}\n336\t\n337\timpl CommandGate {\n338\t    /// Run a single command. Returns `Ok(())` on exit 0, `Err(GateFailure)` otherwise.\n339\t    ///\n340\t    /// The command's environment carries `NEEDLE_BEAD_ID` and `NEEDLE_WORKSPACE`\n341\t    /// so a gate can identify the bead it is judging without racily guessing it\n342\t    /// from external state (e.g. `br list --json` assignee) — see GitHub issue\n343\t    /// jedarden/NEEDLE#7.\n344\t    ///\n345\t    /// Uses `tokio::process::Command` (not `std::process::Command`) with\n346\t    /// `kill_on_drop(true)`: this makes the command a genuine `.await` yield\n347\t    /// point, so `OutcomeHandler::handle_with_cancellation`'s\n348\t    /// `tokio::time::timeout` can actually abandon and kill a gate command\n349\t    /// that outruns `validation.outcome_timeout_seconds`, instead of only\n350\t    /// observing after the fact that it ran too long (see bf-3saat — a\n351\t    /// blocking `std::process::Command` call has no yield point, so a\n352\t    /// wrapping timeout can never preempt it mid-command).\n353\t    async fn run_command(\n354\t        &self,\n355\t        cmd: &str,\n356\t        bead: &crate::types::Bead,\n357\t        workspace: &Path,\n358\t    ) -> std::result::Result<(), GateFailure> {\n359\t        let result = tokio::process::Command::new(\"sh\")\n360\t            .arg(\"-c\")\n361\t            .arg(cmd)\n362\t            .current_dir(workspace)\n363\t            .env(\"NEEDLE_BEAD_ID\", bead.id.to_string())\n364\t            .env(\"NEEDLE_WORKSPACE\", workspace.display().to_string())\n365\t            .kill_on_drop(true)\n366\t            .output()\n367\t            .await;\n368\t\n369\t        match result {\n370\t            Ok(output) => {\n371\t                if output.status.success() {\n372\t                    Ok(())\n373\t                } else {\n374\t                    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n375\t                    let truncated = truncate_output(&stderr, self.stderr_cap_bytes);\n376\t                    Err(GateFailure {\n377\t                        command: cmd.to_string(),\n378\t                        exit_code: output.status.code(),\n379\t                        output: truncated,\n380\t                    })\n381\t                }\n382\t            }\n383\t            Err(e) => Err(GateFailure {\n384\t                command: cmd.to_string(),\n385\t                exit_code: None,\n386\t                output: format!(\"failed to execute command: {}\", e),\n387\t            }),\n388\t        }\n389\t    }\n390\t}\n391\t\n392\t/// Truncate output to at most `max_bytes`, adding an ellipsis if truncated.\n393\tfn truncate_output(s: &str, max_bytes: usize) -> String {\n394\t    if s.len() <= max_bytes {\n395\t        s.to_string()\n396\t    } else {\n397\t        let truncated = &s[..max_bytes];\n398\t        format!(\"{}... [truncated]\", truncated)\n399\t    }\n400\t}\n401\t\n402\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n403\t// ValidationGate (Main Entry Point)\n404\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n405\t\n406\t/// Runs configured verification gates in a workspace directory.\n407\t///\n408\t/// This is the main entry point for validation. It uses the pluggable gate\n409\t/// system to run all configured gates and returns an aggregated report.\n410\tpub struct ValidationGate {\n411\t    gates: Vec<(String, Arc<dyn Gate>)>,\n412\t    workspace: PathBuf,\n413\t}\n414\t\n415\timpl ValidationGate {\n416\t    /// Create a new validation gate from gate configurations.\n417\t    ///\n418\t    /// Returns `None` if `gate_configs` is empty (no verification configured).\n419\t    pub fn new(gate_configs: Vec<(String, GateConfig)>, workspace: PathBuf) -> Option<Self> {\n420\t        if gate_configs.is_empty() {\n421\t            return None;\n422\t        }\n423\t\n424\t        let registry = GateRegistry::global();\n425\t        let mut gates = Vec::new();\n426\t\n427\t        for (name, config) in gate_configs {\n428\t            match registry.create_gate(&config) {\n429\t                Ok(gate) => gates.push((name, gate)),\n430\t                Err(e) => {\n431\t                    tracing::warn!(\n432\t                        gate_name = %name,\n433\t                        error = %e,\n434\t                        \"failed to create gate — skipping\"\n435\t                    );\n436\t                }\n437\t            }\n438\t        }\n439\t\n440\t        if gates.is_empty() {\n441\t            return None;\n442\t        }\n443\t\n444\t        Some(ValidationGate { gates, workspace })\n445\t    }\n446\t\n447\t    /// Create from legacy command list (backwards compatibility).\n448\t    ///\n449\t    /// This method maintains the existing API for code that uses `Vec<String>`\n450\t    /// for verification commands. Uses the default stderr cap (4096 bytes);\n451\t    /// use [`Self::from_commands_with_stderr_cap`] to configure it.\n452\t    pub fn from_commands(commands: Vec<String>, workspace: PathBuf) -> Option<Self> {\n453\t        Self::from_commands_with_stderr_cap(commands, workspace, DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES)\n454\t    }\n455\t\n456\t    /// Create from legacy command list with an explicit stderr capture cap.\n457\t    ///\n458\t    /// See GitHub issue jedarden/NEEDLE#9 — used by\n459\t    /// `OutcomeHandler::handle_success` to apply `validation.stderr_cap_bytes`\n460\t    /// to the legacy `verification:` config format.\n461\t    pub fn from_commands_with_stderr_cap(\n462\t        commands: Vec<String>,\n463\t        workspace: PathBuf,\n464\t        stderr_cap_bytes: usize,\n465\t    ) -> Option<Self> {\n466\t        if commands.is_empty() {\n467\t            return None;\n468\t        }\n469\t        let gate = Arc::new(CommandGate::with_stderr_cap(commands, stderr_cap_bytes));\n470\t        Some(ValidationGate {\n471\t            gates: vec![(\"command_gate\".to_string(), gate as Arc<dyn Gate>)],\n472\t            workspace,\n473\t        })\n474\t    }\n475\t\n476\t    /// Run all gates sequentially. Stops at the first failure.\n477\t    pub async fn run(&self, bead: &crate::types::Bead) -> Result<GateReport> {\n478\t        let mut results = HashMap::new();\n479\t\n480\t        for (name, gate) in &self.gates {\n481\t            let result = gate.validate(bead, &self.workspace).await?;\n482\t            results.insert(name.clone(), result);\n483\t\n484\t            // Stop on first failure.\n485\t            if !results.values().all(|r| r.passed()) {\n486\t                break;\n487\t            }\n488\t        }\n489\t\n490\t        Ok(GateReport::new(results))\n491\t    }\n492\t\n493\t    /// Workspace directory where gates run.\n494\t    pub fn workspace(&self) -> &Path {\n495\t        &self.workspace\n496\t    }\n497\t}\n498\t\n499\t// Validation gates: pre-closure verification.\n//!\n//! After an agent exits successfully (code 0), validation gates run to verify\n//! the work before accepting bead closure. If any gate fails, the bead is\n//! released instead of having its closure accepted.\n//!\n//! # Pluggable Gate System\n//!\n//! Gates implement the [`Gate`] trait and are registered in the [`GateRegistry`].\n//! Built-in gate types:\n//! - `command`: Runs shell commands in the workspace directory\n//!\n//! Custom gates can be registered at runtime by calling [`GateRegistry::register`].\n//!\n//! Inspired by bg-gate (docs/research/bg-gate-validation.md).\n\npub mod predispatch;\nmod shipped_work;\npub use shipped_work::verify_shipped_work;\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};\n\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Types\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Result of a single gate validation.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum GateResult {\n    /// The gate passed validation.\n    Pass,\n    /// The gate failed validation with a reason.\n    Fail(String),\n}\n\nimpl GateResult {\n    /// Returns true if the gate passed.\n    pub fn passed(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, GateResult::Pass)\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the failure reason if this is a `Fail` result.\n    pub fn failure_reason(&self) -> Option<&str> {\n        match self {\n            GateResult::Pass => None,\n            GateResult::Fail(reason) => Some(reason),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Aggregated result of running multiple validation gates.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct GateReport {\n    /// Whether all gates passed.\n    pub all_passed: bool,\n    /// Individual gate results keyed by gate name.\n    pub results: HashMap<String, GateResult>,\n}\n\nimpl GateReport {\n    /// Create a new report from individual gate results.\n    pub fn new(results: HashMap<String, GateResult>) -> Self {\n        let all_passed = results.values().all(|r| r.passed());\n        GateReport {\n            all_passed,\n            results,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a report where all gates passed.\n    pub fn all_pass() -> Self {\n        GateReport {\n            all_passed: true,\n            results: HashMap::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a report with a single gate failure.\n    pub fn single_failure(gate_name: impl Into<String>, reason: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        let mut results = HashMap::new();\n        results.insert(gate_name.into(), GateResult::Fail(reason.into()));\n        GateReport {\n            all_passed: false,\n            results,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Configuration for a single gate from `.needle.yaml`.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"type\", rename_all = \"snake_case\")]\npub enum GateConfig {\n    /// Run shell commands in the workspace directory.\n    Command {\n        commands: Vec<String>,\n        /// Maximum bytes of stderr captured on failure. `None` means \"inherit\n        /// `validation.stderr_cap_bytes`\" — the caller assembling gate configs\n        /// (see `outcome::OutcomeHandler::handle_success`) fills this in from\n        /// the resolved `Config` before construction. See GitHub issue\n        /// jedarden/NEEDLE#9.\n        #[serde(default)]\n        stderr_cap_bytes: Option<usize>,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Validation errors that can occur during bead store validation.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum ValidationError {\n    /// Invalid dependency kind or type.\n    InvalidKind {\n        /// The invalid kind value that was encountered.\n        kind: String,\n    },\n}\n\nimpl std::fmt::Display for ValidationError {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            ValidationError::InvalidKind { kind } => {\n                write!(f, \"invalid kind: '{}'\", kind)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::error::Error for ValidationError {}\n\n/// A single gate command that failed (for backwards compatibility).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct GateFailure {\n    /// The command that was run.\n    pub command: String,\n    /// Process exit code (None if killed by signal).\n    pub exit_code: Option<i32>,\n    /// Combined stderr output (truncated to a reasonable length).\n    pub output: String,\n}\n\n/// Aggregated result of running a `ValidationGate`.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ValidationRunResult {\n    /// Whether all gate commands passed.\n    pub passed: bool,\n    /// List of failures (empty when `passed` is true).\n    pub failures: Vec<GateFailure>,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Gate Trait\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A validation gate that can check bead work before accepting closure.\n///\n/// Gates are run after an agent exits successfully (code 0). If any gate\n/// returns `GateResult::Fail`, the bead is released instead of having its\n/// closure accepted.\n///\n/// `validate` is async so a slow gate is a genuine `.await` yield point:\n/// `OutcomeHandler::handle_with_cancellation`'s `tokio::time::timeout` can\n/// only preempt a future at such a point (see bf-3saat) — a synchronous,\n/// blocking implementation would let the configured\n/// `validation.outcome_timeout_seconds` observe a slow gate only after the\n/// fact, never actually cut it off.\n#[async_trait::async_trait]\npub trait Gate: Send + Sync {\n    /// Validate the bead's work in the given workspace.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `GateResult::Pass` if validation succeeds, or `GateResult::Fail`\n    /// with a human-readable reason if it fails.\n    async fn validate(&self, bead: &crate::types::Bead, workspace: &Path) -> Result<GateResult>;\n\n    /// Gate type name for telemetry and configuration (e.g., \"command\", \"custom\").\n    fn gate_type(&self) -> &str;\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Gate Registry\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Registry for pluggable validation gates.\n///\n/// Gates are registered by type name. The registry is thread-safe and supports\n/// dynamic registration of custom gate types.\npub struct GateRegistry {\n    #[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\n    gates: RwLock<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn Fn(&GateConfig) -> Result<Arc<dyn Gate>> + Send + Sync>>>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for GateRegistry {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl GateRegistry {\n    /// Create a new registry with built-in gate types registered.\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let registry = GateRegistry {\n            gates: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),\n        };\n        // Register built-in gate types\n        registry.register_builtin_gates();\n        registry\n    }\n\n    /// Register a built-in gate type constructor.\n    fn register_builtin_gates(&self) {\n        self.register(\"command\", |config| match config {\n            GateConfig::Command {\n                commands,\n                stderr_cap_bytes,\n            } => Ok(Arc::new(CommandGate::with_stderr_cap(\n                commands.clone(),\n                stderr_cap_bytes.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES),\n            ))),\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Register a custom gate type constructor.\n    ///\n    /// The constructor function takes a `GateConfig` and returns a boxed `Gate`.\n    /// Custom gates should parse their config from the `GateConfig` enum.\n    ///\n    /// # Example\n    /// ```ignore\n    /// registry.register(\"my_gate\", |config| {\n    ///     // Parse config and create custom gate\n    ///     Ok(Arc::new(MyCustomGate::new(config)?))\n    /// });\n    /// ```\n    pub fn register<F>(&self, gate_type: impl Into<String>, constructor: F)\n    where\n        F: Fn(&GateConfig) -> Result<Arc<dyn Gate>> + Send + Sync + 'static,\n    {\n        let gate_type = gate_type.into();\n        let mut guards = self.gates.write().unwrap();\n        guards.insert(gate_type, Arc::new(constructor));\n    }\n\n    /// Create a gate instance from its configuration.\n    ///\n    /// Returns an error if the gate type is not registered or construction fails.\n    pub fn create_gate(&self, config: &GateConfig) -> Result<Arc<dyn Gate>> {\n        let gate_type = match config {\n            GateConfig::Command { .. } => \"command\",\n        };\n\n        let guards = self.gates.read().unwrap();\n        let constructor = guards\n            .get(gate_type)\n            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"unknown gate type '{}': not registered\", gate_type))?;\n\n        constructor(config)\n    }\n\n    /// Get the global gate registry instance.\n    pub fn global() -> &'static GateRegistry {\n        use std::sync::OnceLock;\n        static REGISTRY: OnceLock<GateRegistry> = OnceLock::new();\n        REGISTRY.get_or_init(GateRegistry::new)\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Built-in Gate Types\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Default stderr capture cap (bytes) — preserves the previous hardcoded value.\nconst DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES: usize = 4096;\n\n/// Runs configured shell commands as validation gates.\npub struct CommandGate {\n    commands: Vec<String>,\n    /// Maximum bytes of stderr captured on failure. Configurable via\n    /// `validation.stderr_cap_bytes` — see GitHub issue jedarden/NEEDLE#9.\n    stderr_cap_bytes: usize,\n}\n\nimpl CommandGate {\n    /// Create a new command gate with the default stderr cap (4096 bytes).\n    pub fn new(commands: Vec<String>) -> Self {\n        Self::with_stderr_cap(commands, DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES)\n    }\n\n    /// Create a new command gate with an explicit stderr capture cap.\n    pub fn with_stderr_cap(commands: Vec<String>, stderr_cap_bytes: usize) -> Self {\n        CommandGate {\n            commands,\n            stderr_cap_bytes,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait::async_trait]\nimpl Gate for CommandGate {\n    async fn validate(&self, bead: &crate::types::Bead, workspace: &Path) -> Result<GateResult> {\n        // Run commands sequentially; stop at first failure.\n        for cmd in &self.commands {\n            tracing::info!(\n                command = %cmd,\n                workspace = %workspace.display(),\n                \"running command gate\"\n            );\n\n            match self.run_command(cmd, bead, workspace).await {\n                Ok(()) => {\n                    tracing::info!(command = %cmd, \"command gate passed\");\n                }\n                Err(failure) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        command = %cmd,\n                        exit_code = ?failure.exit_code,\n                        \"command gate failed\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(GateResult::Fail(format!(\n                        \"command '{}' failed: {}\",\n                        cmd,\n                        failure.output.trim()\n                    )));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(GateResult::Pass)\n    }\n\n    fn gate_type(&self) -> &str {\n        \"command\"\n    }\n}\n\nimpl CommandGate {\n    /// Run a single command. Returns `Ok(())` on exit 0, `Err(GateFailure)` otherwise.\n    ///\n    /// The command's environment carries `NEEDLE_BEAD_ID` and `NEEDLE_WORKSPACE`\n    /// so a gate can identify the bead it is judging without racily guessing it\n    /// from external state (e.g. `br list --json` assignee) — see GitHub issue\n    /// jedarden/NEEDLE#7.\n    ///\n    /// Uses `tokio::process::Command` (not `std::process::Command`) with\n    /// `kill_on_drop(true)`: this makes the command a genuine `.await` yield\n    /// point, so `OutcomeHandler::handle_with_cancellation`'s\n    /// `tokio::time::timeout` can actually abandon and kill a gate command\n    /// that outruns `validation.outcome_timeout_seconds`, instead of only\n    /// observing after the fact that it ran too long (see bf-3saat — a\n    /// blocking `std::process::Command` call has no yield point, so a\n    /// wrapping timeout can never preempt it mid-command).\n    async fn run_command(\n        &self,\n        cmd: &str,\n        bead: &crate::types::Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n    ) -> std::result::Result<(), GateFailure> {\n        let result = tokio::process::Command::new(\"sh\")\n            .arg(\"-c\")\n            .arg(cmd)\n            .current_dir(workspace)\n            .env(\"NEEDLE_BEAD_ID\", bead.id.to_string())\n            .env(\"NEEDLE_WORKSPACE\", workspace.display().to_string())\n            .kill_on_drop(true)\n            .output()\n            .await;\n\n        match result {\n            Ok(output) => {\n                if output.status.success() {\n                    Ok(())\n                } else {\n                    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);\n                    let truncated = truncate_output(&stderr, self.stderr_cap_bytes);\n                    Err(GateFailure {\n                        command: cmd.to_string(),\n                        exit_code: output.status.code(),\n                        output: truncated,\n                    })\n                }\n            }\n            Err(e) => Err(GateFailure {\n                command: cmd.to_string(),\n                exit_code: None,\n                output: format!(\"failed to execute command: {}\", e),\n            }),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Truncate output to at most `max_bytes`, adding an ellipsis if truncated.\nfn truncate_output(s: &str, max_bytes: usize) -> String {\n    if s.len() <= max_bytes {\n        s.to_string()\n    } else {\n        let truncated = &s[..max_bytes];\n        format!(\"{}... [truncated]\", truncated)\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// ValidationGate (Main Entry Point)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Runs configured verification gates in a workspace directory.\n///\n/// This is the main entry point for validation. It uses the pluggable gate\n/// system to run all configured gates and returns an aggregated report.\npub struct ValidationGate {\n    gates: Vec<(String, Arc<dyn Gate>)>,\n    workspace: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl ValidationGate {\n    /// Create a new validation gate from gate configurations.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `None` if `gate_configs` is empty (no verification configured).\n    pub fn new(gate_configs: Vec<(String, GateConfig)>, workspace: PathBuf) -> Option<Self> {\n        if gate_configs.is_empty() {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        let registry = GateRegistry::global();\n        let mut gates = Vec::new();\n\n        for (name, config) in gate_configs {\n            match registry.create_gate(&config) {\n                Ok(gate) => gates.push((name, gate)),\n                Err(e) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        gate_name = %name,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"failed to create gate — skipping\"\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if gates.is_empty() {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        Some(ValidationGate { gates, workspace })\n    }\n\n    /// Create from legacy command list (backwards compatibility).\n    ///\n    /// This method maintains the existing API for code that uses `Vec<String>`\n    /// for verification commands. Uses the default stderr cap (4096 bytes);\n    /// use [`Self::from_commands_with_stderr_cap`] to configure it.\n    pub fn from_commands(commands: Vec<String>, workspace: PathBuf) -> Option<Self> {\n        Self::from_commands_with_stderr_cap(commands, workspace, DEFAULT_STDERR_CAP_BYTES)\n    }\n\n    /// Create from legacy command list with an explicit stderr capture cap.\n    ///\n    /// See GitHub issue jedarden/NEEDLE#9 — used by\n    /// `OutcomeHandler::handle_success` to apply `validation.stderr_cap_bytes`\n    /// to the legacy `verification:` config format.\n    pub fn from_commands_with_stderr_cap(\n        commands: Vec<String>,\n        workspace: PathBuf,\n        stderr_cap_bytes: usize,\n    ) -> Option<Self> {\n        if commands.is_empty() {\n            return None;\n        }\n        let gate = Arc::new(CommandGate::with_stderr_cap(commands, stderr_cap_bytes));\n        Some(ValidationGate {\n            gates: vec![(\"command_gate\".to_string(), gate as Arc<dyn Gate>)],\n            workspace,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Run all gates sequentially. Stops at the first failure.\n    pub async fn run(&self, bead: &crate::types::Bead) -> Result<GateReport> {\n        let mut results = HashMap::new();\n\n        for (name, gate) in &self.gates {\n            let result = gate.validate(bead, &self.workspace).await?;\n            results.insert(name.clone(), result);\n\n            // Stop on first failure.\n            if !results.values().all(|r| r.passed()) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(GateReport::new(results))\n    }\n\n    /// Workspace directory where gates run.\n    pub fn workspace(&self) -> &Path {\n        &self.workspace\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// jedarden/NEEDLE#8 follow-up (bf-3saat): the gate command\n        // must be a genuine .await yield point so a wrapping tokio::time::timeout\n        // can actually cancel and kill it, not just observe it after the fact.\n        // Simulate that here directly: race the gate's future against a short\n        // timeout and confirm (a) the timeout wins and (b) the child process\n        // is actually gone afterward, not left running in the background.\n        let marker = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        let marker_path = marker.path().to_path_buf();\n        std::fs::remove_file(&marker_path).ok();\n\n        // Writes the marker file only after a 3s sleep — if the process is\n        // truly killed before then, the marker must never appear.\n        let !marker_path.exists(),\n            \"gate command was not actually killed — it ran to completion in the background\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    // ── ValidationGate tests ──\n\n    #[test]\n    fn
